But yeah, just staying concentrated on the actual task doesn't really seem to be the issue.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself constantly worrying about making mistakes, even when you're not actively working on a project?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, I'm not sitting around at home stressing about it when I'm off the clock. I might get annoyed when I realize I did something wrong, but it's not like this constant worry hanging over my head all the time. Once I'm done working, I'm mostly just moving on to the next thing.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any difficulty remembering small details or keeping track of steps in a process, even if you're paying attention?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I have a hard time remembering things like that. I know the steps to edit a sequence, and I don't forget how to do it or lose track of what I'm supposed to be doing next. I remember the details fine, I just... I guess I just rush through them sometimes and make a mistake, but it's not because I forgot how to do it or lost my place.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any difficulty maintaining your focus over longer periods or shifting your attention between tasks, even if you’re not feeling distracted by other things?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't really noticed that. And as for shifting attention... no, I don't think I struggle with that either. If I need to stop cutting a scene to grab some different footage or answer a question from a producer, I can switch gears just fine. It doesn't feel like a struggle to move from one task to the next.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself acting quickly or making decisions without fully thinking them through, like skipping steps or rushing to finish something before double-checking?\n\nPatient: I mean, not really.
